# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-11-12)

## Corpus Check
- 5748 files · ~12,607,033 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 118 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 35, .css 18, .entitlements 12)

## Summary
- 78262 nodes · 223587 edges · 2090 communities (1437 shown, 653 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 9922 edges (avg confidence: 0.87)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `fb5abd66`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
